## 2026-10-16 ~23:20 MYT, SNET: camera formula with smooth zoom and dissolve fallback
- Owner's rule written as a formula (`cameraPlan.ts`, `CAMERA_RULES`): each named country is reached 1.0 s before it is said (eased move of 1.2 s), the camera stays until the next country's move (moves at least 0.8 s apart), and returns to the default view 2.0 s after the last country was said, smoothly. `GoogleMapCanvas` now animates frame by frame (ease in-out cubic, Mercator interpolation, target fitted left of the presenter) instead of a fitBounds jump; with `prefers-reduced-motion` it dissolves (fade to the map colour, cut, fade back). When the clip ends by itself the return waits for the formula's return time. Welcome mentions: Singapore 7.8 s, Malaysia 9.0 s, Brunei 10.0 s (estimated, not measured), giving moves at 6.8, 8.0 and 9.0 s and the return at 12.7 s; bulletins use the country segments. ## 2026-10-17 ~02:20 MYT, SNET: chat helper formulation and stress test, map control, nearby help, mini cards, GCP render finished, consultancy briefing
- **Chat helper (SOP AEG-SOP-006).** Behaviour is now the formulation `AEG.CHAT.HELPER` (content/chat/helper.formulation.json, loaded by src/chat/helperFormulation.ts): scope, fixed polite refusal, rules, map rules, nearby rules, AEGIS facts. The model returns scope in or out; an "out" request always gets the fixed refusal. Model for testing: reAPI gpt-6-luna (Luna 6), through the gateway budget. New tools: `aegis_show_on_map` (src/services/mapFocus.ts: alert level, then earthquake size, then affected-people estimate, then recency; current incidents; explains how it chose) and `aegis_find_nearby` (hospital, clinic, pharmacy, police, fire station, disaster office, shelter). Reply blocks `mapFocus` and `places`; the page zooms and opens `IncidentFocus` and `NearbyPlaces`; every link in a reply is a clickable mini card that opens the full card or the map (owner request). Bug found and fixed on the way: the canonical hash takes integers only, so decimal coordinates in blocks are hashed as text.
- **Stress test.** `content/chat/helper.eval.json` (87 cases) and `tools/chat-stress.ts` against the running server and the real model, cost gate #34 (0.24 USD). Round 1 (formulation 1.1.0): 69 of 79 passed, 8 provider problems. Findings: the reAPI content filter answers HTTP 400 to jailbreak-style prompts and three such answers had opened the gateway circuit for everyone (a user could have switched the chat off for 30 s). Fixes: a deterministic pre-filter (src/chat/helperGuards.ts) answers such requests with the polite refusal without a model call; HTTP 400 no longer counts against the circuit; a provider rejection becomes a polite refusal. The model asked "which area?" instead of searching: the request now says whether the map centre is known. Some case patterns were wrong (curly apostrophes, "record no"). Round 2 (formulation 1.2.0): **87 of 87**. Round 2 also showed the real nearby search failing (public Overpass servers at capacity: 2 of 28 live queries answered).
- **Nearby help.** Mirror failover across three public Overpass servers; because they stay unreliable, a stored copy of OpenStreetMap essential services (migration 014 `essential_places`, `tools/fetch-essential-places.ts`, resumable, ODbL credit) answers first; the fetch was started and hospital tiles have loaded (70 + 185 + 177 + 83 so far). Reuse entry for OpenStreetMap in AEG-PRV-001 (licence page not re-read: UNVERIFIED).

The owner's proposed JSON is close but NOT Google's real format: pollutant concentration is `units` not `unit` (the pin card would show PM2.5 as a dash), rain is `precipitation.qpf.quantity` plus `unit: MILLIMETERS` (no `qpfRain`), the condition is `weatherCondition {description {text}, type}` (not a string), the AQI sits inside `indexes[]` with `code, displayName, category` (not a flat aqi), Universal AQI is higher-is-better so 54 is moderate in a different way, and there is no `place_id` in the responses (`source` and `compliance_status` would be our own wrapper). - 2026-10-18 SNET claims AEG-API-STATUS-111 (owner: an API status tab, catalogued): new tab 'API status' in the Admin Desk (pageTemplates.ts adminSections, AdminRoute.tsx), new ApiStatusView.tsx, new src/services/apiStatus.ts, new route GET /api/admin/api-status (administrators only), admin.css. Three groups: Google AI and cloud (Gemini 4 Argon on Vertex AI, Google Cloud Translation, Google Cloud Text-to-Speech, Gemini API chat, backup model reAPI, Cloud Billing export), Google air and weather (scheduled refresh, click-to-fetch, and a row the page makes itself for Google Maps in the browser), and Live public data feeds (USGS, GDACS, NEA PSI, MetMalaysia forecast and warnings, OpenAQ, Open-Meteo; the demo SYNTH feeds are left out). Each row: state (Working, Configured, Off, Not set, Problem), what it is for, Billed or Free or Free allowance, last success, last problem, a one-line detail and the setting that controls it. States come only from what the server already knows: the running configuration, the last finished or failed job of that kind (job_sheet) and each feed's last poll; a feed polled OK within 6 hours is Working, older is only Configured, a failed poll is a Problem with its reason; a configured service with no proof is Configured, never Working. The page makes NO call to any outside service (no cost, no quota), shows no key or secret (only whether one is set and its setting name), and says Google Maps in the browser cannot be tested from the server. Live local result: 10 working, 3 configured, 0 problem, 2 off (Gemini API chat is Off because the primary provider is reAPI; scheduled Google air and weather is Off). Tests: tests/unit/api-status.test.ts (4), tests/browser/api-status.spec.ts (outside-host check included), admin-desk spec tab list updated; vitest and browser specs passed. - 2026-10-18 SNET claims AEG-API-SWITCH-112 (owner: last called timestamp, billed or free, description, and an on/off switch for the APIs that can be switched): migration 023_api_switch.sql (one row per service: off, who, when); new src/services/apiSwitches.ts (SWITCHABLE: vertex-argon, translation, tts, billing-export, google-on-demand; setSwitch; withSwitches); createApp now wraps the deps with withSwitches, so a switched-off service reads as ABSENT for every route (deps.reviewer, tts, translator, billing, envOnDemand are undefined while off), exactly as if it were not set up; new route PUT /api/admin/api-switch {id, on} (administrators only; refuses an unknown id, a service with no switch such as the chat providers, a service that is not set up on this server, and a missing true or false); the status route reads the real services through deps.unswitched. UI (ApiStatusView.tsx): 'Last called' above 'Last success' on every row, and an On/Off switch (role switch) under the state badge on a row that has one; a refusal is shown. Not switchable here, by design: the chat providers (set by AEGIS_PRIMARY_PROVIDER and the keys), the scheduled Google air and weather refresh (its own toggle in Feeds and telemetry) and the live feeds (AEGIS_LIVE_FEEDS, all together). Tests: tests/integration/api-switch.test.ts (3), tests/unit/api-status.test.ts (7), db.test migration list, api-status.spec.ts (2, with the switch mocked), all passing; vitest all green. Live check on the local server: the billing export was switched off and straight back on (now On, Configured); Argon, Translation, Text-to-Speech, Billing export and Click-to-fetch show a switch. Note for the owner: the click-to-fetch switch stops the credits route that does the lookup; the environment status route still reads the service as set up at start, so its own status line may still say it is available while lookups are refused.
